**§2.1 `.tap-floor` — ADOPT-OR-DELETE, decided ONCE for the whole demo: DELETE.** Measured: ⟨`grep -rn 'tap-floor' demo/ test/`⟩ → **2 hits / 1 file** — `design-idioms.css:81` (its own comment, *"the WCAG 2.5.5 44px minimum touch-target floor"*) + `:82` (its selector; body `min-height: 44px; min-width: 44px`, unconditional, not coarse-gated) → **0 adopters**, reproducing the Baseline. Premise-correction lock honoured: the comment's *"44px house practice"* is a DEAD premise (and is what dies with the rule — K2: rule + comment ONE commit); the survivors are **the SC 2.5.8 boundary math (24×24 CSS px + the spacing exception)** and **the shipping 32px answer** (`SharePopover.vue:31-39`, `Button size="sm" emphasis="quiet" class="h-8 w-8 p-0"`), which the ruling names as the demo's exemplar for icon-only controls. Why DELETE and not ADOPT: (i) every W6-E target-size cure in the spec routes to a **producer-size mechanism** (`size`-driven `Button`/`Input` for SP-8/-9/-11; the S-7 reshell owning CopyButton's box for KF-CB-5/-14; the one `min-block-size` policy for D-8) — none routes to a class floor; (ii) the producer ships its own coarse floor **per component** through `--touch-target` (`.glass-chip--interactive`, `.dock-icon-button`, `.tags-input__chip`), so a demo-authored unconditional 44px class is a **shadow of `--touch-target`'s expression** — RETIRED in the disposition table (§2.2), never PREFIXED; (iii) after KF-SST-30 voids `touch-hit-area`, `.tap-floor` was *"the only working in-reach mechanism"* only in the sense that it existed — with zero adopters it worked on nothing, and adopting it would author the dead 44px premise into 7+ files. **Consequences, by row**: KF-CB-25 (fold, zero adopters) DISCHARGED by the delete · CubeScene MISS-3's `.tap-floor` liveness question ANSWERED: DEAD (its trigger disposition stays with the KF-APP-17 sweep) · KSC D-20 + RR-D missed-M4 · KF-KE-45 · KC-23 · KF-KC-33 · SP-11: the class is not their cure; their targets meet the floor by control size, and **the KC-23/KF-KE-45 24px gapless destructive pair is measured against SC 2.5.8's spacing exception at SS-13** — if it fails, the cure is rhythm/gap (KF-KE-40's limb), never a class. **Spent by unit `.e`** (the `design-idioms.css:81-82` strike, inside the disposition-table commit) — G-W6-4's *"fails if `.tap-floor` is left undecided"* bound is discharged by this ruling; G-W6-7's *"fails on any 44px house practice cite"* bound is honoured by deleting the only such cite. Recorded onward → KF.W10 (the `.tap-floor` ruling, §Sequencing → KF.W10).

**§2.2 The disposition-table SKELETON — MISSED-H folded FIRST (heads family D; unit `.e` fills it).** Row 0, the fold, verbatim from the bank and confirmed at the bytes: **the demo DELIBERATELY shadows glass-ui's namespace** — `design-idioms.css:9-11` ⟨re-read⟩: *"OUTSIDE @layer so the demo's copy overrides glass-ui's incidental same-named tokens (authoritative for the demo)"* — so **RETAINED-BY-POLICY is the DEFAULT for the signal-token block**; **PREFIXED requires a SHOWN two-producer collision with a measured consequence** (G-W6-5's framing lock) and is never blanket; **`--amiga-red` is never prefixed** (Canvas2D ignores an invalid `fillStyle` → a solid-white ball with no error). Columns: `property/class` · `sites (files)` · `producer twin (where)` · `disposition ∈ {RETAINED-BY-POLICY · PREFIXED · RETIRED · NO-SURFACE}` (KF-CE-22's third column mandatory) · `lock / rider` · `banked ids`. **Seed rows whose disposition a LOCK already fixes** (the seat fills the remainder from the 124-property census): `--rainbow-*` **RETAINED-BY-POLICY** (I-32 A-13: the documented override surface; SquareScene D-23's crash story is a recorded NEGATIVE) · `--amiga-red` **RETAINED-BY-POLICY** (MISSED-H) · `.tap-floor` **RETIRED** (§2.1) · `.btn-interactive` **RETIRED** (phantom utility, the 8-site sweep WHOLE + `RIBBON_BUTTON_CLASS`) · `--dock-margin` **NO-SURFACE today → DEFINE-OR-DELETE the chain** (KF-APP-6 BLOCKER; a per-site fallback IS the masking; identity-guard — cite the bank) · `--shadow-glass` **RETIRED → reads `--glass-shadow-quiet`/`-resting`**, never the bridge (KF-APP-25 ≡ KF-SKEL-2; I-32 A-14 confirms) · `--dock-label-padding-inline` **RETIRED → `--dock-trigger-padding-inline`** (KF-APP-26) · `.focus-ring` **PREFIXED** (§2.4) · `--specular` / `--shade` **NO-SURFACE → DEFINED demo-side as `light-dark()` pairs** (SequencePlayhead C-3 + D-7 residue, ONE cure; O-26 R-15 gives glass the collision notice) · KF-SKEL-9's nine stage classes → decided AT THE EMITTER (adopt-or-delete, `.k`). **Rows the seat decides at the bytes under the fold**: `.text-gold`/`--color-gold` (RB-2) · `--scale-hover` (RB-5 ≡ EH-10, zero-delta) · `icon-*` (ChromeDock D-5, +1 rung) · `shadow-lg` ×3 (DGC M-4) · `--panel-max-h` (KSM R-21, the semantic collision) · `--controls-idle-opacity` + `@property --rail-width` (CPW D-m3 ≡ ACG D-12) · the `--color-*` triplet (KF-SKEL-17) · `--start-hero-band` (KF-EST-16) · KF-AX-25 / CubeTarget #30's `@property` initial-values · THP `D-17`'s three alphas (joint with THP `D-5`) · THP `D-11`'s 6 px (paired with TimelineTrack `m-17`) · KF-KE-39/-40/-42/-59's radius vocabulary · **script-side constants** (KF-CB-27 — the audit is not a CSS-only sweep) · SPF-32 priced at ZERO spend. The table is the product; no blanket rename exists in this wave.

**§2.3 The S-7 and S-9 DISCHARGE LISTS, stated in writing (gate condition on G-W6-9; unit `.i` verifies each at the bytes and NAMES any it cannot discharge before spending).**
- **S-7 (CopyButton reshell on glass `Button`, the `SharePopover.vue:31-39` exemplar)** — **DISCHARGES**: `KF-CB-7` (the reshell) · `KF-CB-5` + `KF-CB-14` (the zero intrinsic box — `Button` owns the box) · `KF-CB-12` (focus affordance — `Button` self-applies `.focus-ring`) · `KF-CB-13` (three improvised hover dialects → the house lift) · `KF-CB-20` · `KF-CB-21` · `KF-CB-26`. **NOT discharged**: `KF-CB-25` (ruled at §2.1, not by S-7) · `KF-CB-27` (motion constants → the audit) · `KF-CB-37` (the MOVE — KF.W8's; R-13: the reshell modifies IN PLACE) · the four sites' geometry (`KF-ET-17` ≡ `KF-KC-33` ≡ `KC-23`: the box is S-7's, the GAP is SS-13's). **Constraint**: a real `<button>` stays in the DOM (S-2) or CopyButton leaves the roving-tabindex cohort.
- **S-9 (`Textarea` swap, CPD R-5, the densest single cure)** — **KILLS**: `R-1` · `R-6` · `R-8` · `R-9` · `R-12` · `R-14` · `R-20` (+ the KAD folds riding their twins: `KAD-F1` → R-2 · `KAD-F4` → R-20 · `KAD-F5` → R-10). **DISCHARGED WITH THE SWAP ONLY IF THE NAMED ACT LANDS IN THE SAME COMMIT**: `R-2` (a scroll OWNER — `scroll` on `DialogContent`, all three mounts) · `R-26` (`sm:max-w-2xl` + the overflow owner) · `R-25` (the THIRD widget, `KeyframeCard.vue:43` — a fold touching only the two dialogs leaves it) · `R-10` · `R-11` · `R-13` · `R-15` · `R-16` (`icon-md` — the audit names the class) · `R-18`. **NOT discharged**: `R-27` (focus landing — an SS-13 witness; escalates R-1) · `R-24` (the doctrine row; its cure is `--input-on-glass`, a one-line correction that does not wait) · `R-9`'s hardware witness (KF.W9) · `KAD-12` (registry adoption — its own exact cure) · `KAD-6` (STANDS ALONE — W7's R-7 twin fold undeclined by absence at this clock). **KAD cluster MOOTED by the well swap**: `KAD-3` · `KAD-7` · `KAD-19` · `KAD-16` · `KAD-F1/-F4/-F5` · `KAD-17`'s `collapseToEnd`/`insertTabAtCursor` limbs — stated here; `.i`/`.k` confirm at bytes. **S-9's other members**: `LP-8 ≡ KF-CO-35 + ME-18` is an EVALUATION of `/number-field` (never a mechanical swap; TimelineCaret C-4/D·M-9 its third site — MISS-α6 discharges free IF the swap is taken) · the DGC toast verdict is CENSUS-OWNED (recorded, not re-adjudicated). **S-10**: `KF-KE-21 (+KAD-15)` glass `Progress` EVALUATE (bind to something true or delete; KFED-UNIT owns the binding) · **`KSM C-9` `/command`: evaluate-not-replace CONFIRMED and RECORDED here** — the census stops re-raising it; the slot id awaits KF.W0's C-17 mint.

**§2.4 KF-KE-30 — the `.focus-ring` REALIZED class-name collision: resolved BEFORE G-W6-6 asserts (heads family F): the demo's shadow is PREFIXED.** Measured: glass ships `.focus-ring:focus-visible { outline: none; border-radius: var(--radius-pill); box-shadow: var(--focus-ring-shadow); }` — **it binds `--radius-pill` onto the HOST**; the demo's unlayered same-name rule `design-idioms.css:76-78` paints only `box-shadow: var(--focus-ring-shadow)` (no radius) and is consumed at **4 rectangular hosts** ⟨`grep -rn focus-ring demo`⟩ → `SquareScene.vue:46` (`demo-box`) · `SpringTarget.vue:63` (`spring-rail`) · `SpringHeatmap.vue:30` (`rounded-md`) · `KeyframeCard.vue:45` (the code well, `rounded-lg`), plus two direct `--focus-ring-shadow` consumers (`SequenceTarget.css:165`, `playback-idiom.css:73`) that are NOT collisions. Adopting the producer's class as shipped would flip four rectangular hosts to pill on focus (a shape regression); fighting the shared name is the unlayered-shadow defect itself. **Ruling**: the demo's rule is renamed to a demo-owned class (PREFIXED — the ONE class rename this wave makes, under G-W6-5's shown-collision test), its four hosts re-pointed in the same commit (K2), `--focus-ring-shadow` stays the ONE ring token (glass's); glass `Button`/`Input` hosts keep the producer's `.focus-ring` untouched; **`field-control` is never erased** (root-styling law; KF-KC-4/-13's cure is the bare-variant REQUEST — O-26 R-16 — not per-instance erasure); `aria-selected` is not a vehicle; **K-9 inversion holds**: `.focus-ring` (the demo's renamed ring, or the producer's on producer hosts) lands FIRST at TimelineCaret and discharges D·M-5 without waiting on the NumberField evaluation. **Spent by unit `.f`** (design-idioms.css + the four hosts are in its writable set; `SquareScene.vue`/`SpringTarget.vue`/`SpringHeatmap.vue` are `.g`'s — **the rename crosses the `.f`∥`.g` disjointness: `.f` renames the RULE and `KeyframeCard.vue:45`; `.g` re-points its three scene hosts in ITS commit, both under this one ruling — a two-commit landing of one rename is permitted here because the file graph forbids one commit, and the interval between them leaves three hosts ringless, so `.g` lands FIRST or the two seats land within one sitting; the closing seat `.l` measures 0 bare `focus-ring` demo hosts outside producer components**).

**§2.5 ONE FadingScroll decision (CC-N-2 + KSM R-2/R-3/R-4/R-22): EXTEND the shipped two-axis adoption; 2 → 3 mounts.** Opened, as §Sequencing 8 orders, by reading the demo's OWN adoption ⟨`grep -n FadingScroll demo -r`⟩ → `EasingTarget.vue:42` `<FadingScroll axis="x" class="family-filter">` · `:76` `<FadingScroll axis="y" class="specimen-drawer min-h-0 w-full flex-1">` · `:137` `import { FadingScroll } from "@mkbabb/glass-ui/fading-scroll"` (+ the PROSE site `ChannelControls.vue:51`). **(a) KSM**: `FadingScroll axis="y"` as the named TABBABLE scroll port inside `DialogContent` (`KeyboardShortcutsModal.vue:3`, `class="max-w-md"`) **PLUS `scroll` on `DialogContent`** — the verified composition; baseline posture = the `:76` mount (flex-column child, `min-h-0 flex-1`, default mask and gutter); **the written departure**: the port gains `tabindex="0"` + an accessible name, because the drawer precedent is pointer-scrolled inside a scene while the modal's port must be keyboard-reachable in every engine (R-2's defect) — a knowing departure, recorded here. Retires R-2/R-3/R-4/R-22 + the RTL/gutter residue; the KSM composition pass (R-5..R-20) rides on top of this import. **(b) ChannelControls' strip fade (CC-N-2)**: the hand-rolled fade DIES with the branch (S-1 DELETE); **no FadingScroll is mounted in its place** — *"adopt-the-vendor-primitive where a fade is wanted"* and no fade is wanted on a deleted strip; the `:51-54` prose dies under K2. **Spent by `.i` (a) and `.c` (b).**

**§2.6 ONE glass-tier decision (KF-ET-21 + KF-ES-21): the HOST floats, the CONTENTS do not.** Measured: the demo's own tier vocabulary is thin ⟨`grep -rho 'glass-floating\|glass-plate…\|glass-quiet\|glass-wash' demo`⟩ → `glass-plate` 1 · `glass-quiet` 2 · `glass-wash` 4; the 28 floating-tier plates in the easing drawer come from the **Chip itself** (`glass-capsule` + `glass-chip` + `glass-fill-*`, producer-composed), and the sidebar stacks three translucent surfaces. **Ruling**: at most ONE backdrop-filter tier per scene host — the scene's plate/drawer is the floating tier; specimens inside a masked scroller render CONTENT-tier (no per-item backdrop-filter, no second wash); nested `.glass-wash` collapses to one rung (CC-D-9). Where the Chip cannot be told so consumer-side (its tier is producer-composed — measured), the consumer lever is **R-9's ToggleGroup-reshell arm** (real `<button>`s on a content tier) and the producer counterparty is **O-26 R-10's material opt-out**; the sidebar's three surfaces collapse to host + content. **Spent by `.i`** (`EasingTarget.vue`) with `.g` for `EasingSidebar.vue`/`EasingScene.vue`; the SS-6 bound holds — no demo-side override of the Chip's tier classes.

**§2.7 ONE canvas-colour discipline (G-W6-11): adopt the producer's `./canvas` (`resolveCanvasColor` + `useCanvas2D`) with `onFlipSettled`.** Measured: `./canvas` is a published subpath with **0 demo consumers** (Baseline); demo `fillStyle` sites ⟨`grep -rn fillStyle demo`⟩ → `amiga/utils.ts:41/:50` (through the demo's OWN hand-rolled `resolveColor`, whose `:9` comment states the mechanism *"fillStyle does NOT resolve var(…)"*), `amiga/useAmigaThree.ts:72`, `SpringHeatmap.vue:170` (a `color-mix(…)` string). Of the three admissible shapes: **`@property <color>` registration is REJECTED as the discipline** (KF-AX-25 / CubeTarget #30: document-wide registration + `initial-value` poisons every fallback — the audit's own finding); **C-M-4's CSS-gradient dissolution is a per-site shape, not a discipline** (available where a site's geometry allows, never the rule); **the producer's resolver + `useCanvas2D` + `onFlipSettled` re-bake is the ONE discipline** — the demo's `resolveColor` is RETIRED into it (one resolver, the producer's), every canvas re-bakes after the flip settles (D-M2's dark→light race), AmigaScene's packet re-bakes under the same hook (the stage-plate recipe is EVALUATE-NOT-SWAP per the `:6-12` bound). **Escalation stays armed with `.g`**: D-M3 → BLOCKER iff SS-13 probe #1 lands branch (a). Filed as design defects, not WCAG 1.4.11. **Spent by `.g`.**

**§2.8 ONE `min-block-size` policy (KeyframeTimeline D-8 + SequenceScene D22 + KF-SST-39): no demo `h-*` on a glass CONTROL; height is `size`-driven; container minimums are layout and stay.** Measured: `KeyframeTimeline.vue:108` `class="font-mono text-admin-label h-6 w-32"` on an `Input` and `:115` `class="h-6 w-6 p-0"` on a `Button` — `h-*` overrides `height` but not the producer's `min-block-size`, and the `h-6` Input renders 24 px, 33% under the smallest published control (bank, re-verified in the installed dist). **Ruling**: (i) a glass control's block size comes from its `size` prop and the producer's `min-block-size`; **every `h-*` on a glass control is struck** and the wanted rung is requested by `size`; where no published rung is short enough, the ask is a producer size rung, never a demo override; (ii) **container minimums are a different thing and are permitted** (`StartingStyleTarget.vue:136` `min-height: 7rem` is a layout floor on a container, not a control override — RETAINED); (iii) the Card-family arm (KF-SST-39 / D22) folds to the same two clauses. **Spent by `.f`** (KeyframeTimeline) and `.k`/`.g` (the folds).

**§2.9 ONE provider policy (KF-APP-21 ≡ ACG D-10/C-5/L-9): CONFIG UNIFICATION at the ROOT, NOT deletion.** Measured ⟨`grep -rn TooltipProvider demo`⟩ → **three providers**: `App.vue:8` root `<TooltipProvider>` (default delays, i.e. reka's 700 ms; its `:3-5` comment records that every dock/transport/controls tooltip *"must be used within TooltipProvider"* or the route blanks — **LOAD-BEARING**) · `AnimationControlsGroup.vue:2` and `ChannelControls.vue:2`, both `:delay-duration="100" :skip-delay-duration="0"`. **Ruling**: the root provider carries the ONE config — `delay-duration=100`, `skip-delay-duration=0` (the value two authors independently chose; 700 ms was chosen by nobody) — and the two nested providers are DELETED (their imports with them); the root is never deleted. **Spent by `.c`** (ChannelControls/ACG are in its set; `App.vue` modify-carve grants the provider policy by name).

**§2.10 ONE register per role (W6-G's discipline) — the ROLE → REGISTER map, decided once; unit `.h` executes it at the bytes.** (a) **user-typed input** → a non-uppercasing register at a non-zooming size (`text-mono-small` size-driven, or the ruled clamp — the repo's own 16 px iOS floor); never `text-admin-label` / `text-mono-caption` on an input (KeyframeTimeline D-9 · CPD R-9 ≡ SP-4 · SP-7 — SP-4 + SP-7 ONE edit; **D-9's MAJOR is NOT spent while N-2 is unruled**, §Sequencing 5). (b) **code / identifier / artifact surfaces** → mono, case-PRESERVING (`text-transform: none`), the `KeyframeCard.vue:45` fix as the template (KAD-4 · KAD-8 · KF-KE-23 · KF-SS-4 ≡ KF-ES-8's extension · KF-SS-9's four registers → one). (c) **numeric readouts / data** → Mono-as-data, tabular (TimelineTrack D-3 · TimelineCaret m-2/C-6(a) · M10's size half). (d) **UI labels and headings** → the TEXT face, never mono (KSM R-6/R-7 — `design-idioms.css:222-231`'s `.status-badge` is the in-tree kill-shot; KF-CO-30/-25). (e) **tooltip body** → `text-mono-small` UNDER MISSED-3 (the `max-h-24` box resizes or drops in the SAME commit; THP D-4; the `m-17`/`D-11` padding pair lands with it). (f) **hero display** → `--font-display-weight` read at `:root`, NEVER `@theme` (KF-AT-22's cure-discrimination rider; SquareInstrument D-20's eight selectors = two re-points to `--type-weight-display` + `--type-tracking-display`; the three stale comments die in their commits). (g) **italic (KF-SCR-2 ≡ KF-EST-2, the ONE font decision)**: the demo does not synthesize (`style.css:100` stays); an italic that cannot paint is **RETIRED from the demo's register** and replaced by a real differentiator (THP MISSED-2), the `:32-33` comments corrected in the same commit; the producer question (ship a face / declare the dependency) rides **O-26 R-12** and can only ADD a face later, never re-open this ruling. **`normal-case` ALWAYS pairs `tracking-normal` (MM-29)** at all five shipped sites. **KF-SCR-2 stays KF-AV-28-GOVERNED** on `SequenceScrubber.vue` — `.h` states the (absent) SWAP verdict before spending.

**§2.11 W6-AUTH-1, RE-RULED on I-32 A-7 and the 7.0.0 measurement — the migration target for KPT-SUP-4.** The export ask is WITHDRAWN as answered; **the published door at 7.0.0 is `useSelectionGroup` on `./motion-core`** (composes `useTabRovingFocus` verbatim; price: `model` + `containerRef` required, `select` recenters). **Consequences**: (a) CC-C-6's successor formula for a MOUNTED tablist stays `SegmentedTabs semantics="tabs" variant="pill"` — but S-1 is DELETE, not replace, so **no successor strip is mounted in this wave**; (b) the *"retained local composable pending the export ask"* arm is REPLACED: the seam's roving-focus contract migrates onto **`useSelectionGroup` via `./motion-core`** (the published door), never the private `useTabRovingFocus-*.js` chunk (a deep import still FAILS G-W6-2); (c) the 7-case gate `test/demo/instrument/KfPillTabs.test.ts` (201 L, 7 `it(`) MIGRATES onto that door carrying (i) `rovingValue`'s four-line contract · (ii) L:D-14's host-shape criterion · (iii) D:D-21's forced-colors reservation · (iv) missed-M6's untested click path · (v) the getter-thunk params idiom — preserved or replaced DELIBERATELY. **Spent by `.c`**, which picks the door before the composable file is removed (§Sequencing 4). The bank's half-false cell (`kf-ChannelControls.md:50`, D-INV-2) is owed as E-3 addendum **(iii)** — flagged upward, unchanged here.

#### §1 · Act 1 — LOCK (b): the T.G9 warm re-homes BEFORE the branch dies

**Measured first.** The warm hung off `KfPillTabs.vue`'s `@pointerenter`/`@focusin` at `ChannelControls.vue:79-80`, inside the `v-if="!tabsExternallyManaged"` host — ⟨`git grep -n 'TABS_EXTERNALLY_MANAGED_KEY' -- demo/`⟩ → provided `true` unconditionally at `App.vue:168`, injected with default `false` at `ChannelControls.vue:281`, and ⟨`git grep -n 'EditorShell' -- demo/`⟩ shows `App.vue:33` is the ONLY mount path — so the interaction half of T.G9 fired for nobody and the pane waited on `useKeyframesPaneReveal`'s idle warm alone (CC-L-6's *"contract void"*, reproduced).

**Cure, at the host the lock names.** `ChromeDock.vue`'s controls `<Select>` trigger gains `@pointerenter`/`@focusin` → `warmControlSurfaces()`, a Vite dynamic-import warmup of `@components/instrument/keyframes/KeyframesStringControls.vue` with the rejection swallowed — **the shape the shipped scene warm already uses**, ⟨`sed -n '118,123p' demo/app/scene/scenes.ts`⟩ → `export function warmScene(id) { const loader = sceneLoaders.get(id); if (loader) void loader().catch(() => {}); }`. The in-file precedent for a dock-side warm listener is `ChromeDock.vue:263`'s own `@pointerenter="emit('warmScene', scene.id)"` on a `SelectItem`. The pane's `keyframesWarmed` flag still flips on the idle warm or the instant the surface is selected; what the re-home restores is the PREFETCH, so the select-time mount lands on a warm module cache — the property T.G9's own docblock claims (*"the editor still opens instantly on tab-select"*).

**Why this shape and not a prop or an emit**: a `warmKeyframes` handle lives per-`ChannelControls` instance, four levels below the dock; threading it up is prop-drilling through `EditorShell` → `AnimationControlsGroup` → `ControlsPaneWrapper`, and an emit with no handler is a no-op dressed as a cure. One file, zero drilling, the house idiom.

#### §0 · LOCK R4-1 — the three limbs, ONE commit

Census **A-11** is reproduced at the base bytes before the cut, ⟨`git grep -n 'EditorHeader' 98be70f5 -- demo/ test/ src/`⟩ → **2 files, 2 lines**:

```
98be70f5:demo/components/instrument/shell/index.ts:2:export { default as EditorHeader } from "./EditorHeader.vue";
98be70f5:demo/styles/layout.css:15:    --header-items-max-w: 500px; /* the EditorHeader expanded items-wrapper cap … */
```

— i.e. **ONE live importer** (the barrel), **exported-symbol consumers ∅**, **mounts 0** ⟨`git grep -n '<EditorHeader' 98be70f5 -- demo/`⟩ → 0, and the coupled token whose **sole consumer** is the fork's own scoped rule ⟨`git grep -n 'header-items-max-w' 98be70f5 -- demo/`⟩ → **3 lines / 2 files**, two of them inside `EditorHeader.vue` (`:90` its naming comment, `:92` `max-width: var(--header-items-max-w)`). The census's "not yet orphan" reading is therefore exact: the token is orphaned **by** the delete, in the same act, which is why the limbs may not split.

**`4b909ed2`** — `refactor(kf/w6.d)!: the EditorHeader fork dies WITH its barrel edge and the token that would outlive it — ONE ACT, three limbs (KF-APP-41 adopted, RULINGS-4 R4-1; G-W6-15)`

⟨`git show --stat --format='' 4b909ed2`⟩:

```
 demo/components/instrument/shell/EditorHeader.vue | 108 ----------------------
 demo/components/instrument/shell/index.ts         |   1 -
 demo/styles/layout.css                            |   1 -
 3 files changed, 110 deletions(-)
```

Limb 1 = the 108-line file deleted; limb 2 = `shell/index.ts:2` struck (**R4-5(c): KF.W6 is the barrel's ONE write owner; KF.W8 G7 measures and performs no barrel edit** — that ownership is exercised here and nowhere else in this unit); limb 3 = `layout.css:15` struck **whole**, declaration *and* its naming comment together (KF-CE-41 — a comment naming a dead component is the same phantom the declaration is).

**Procedural note, recorded because it nearly forced a forbidden command.** `git add <path>` on the deleted file fails — `git rm` had already staged it as `D` and it is absent from the worktree, so the pathspec matches nothing. The lawful shape is to `git add` **only the two modified paths** and let the commit's **own pathspec** carry all three, which records the deletion: `git commit --no-verify -- <the three paths>`. **`git add -A` / `-u` was not reached for** (four tracks share this index).

#### §1 · EH-4 — ONE row, BOTH spend sites, one commit

**`998124e1`** — the row is LIVE at **two** banked anchors (`EditorShell.vue:45` and `MbabbMenu.vue:20`); LAW F(3) forbids disposing the second by the first's green, and the spec's commit families forbid splitting one row across two commits. Both `title="Toggle dark mode"` attributes are dropped in one act.

**Measured at the producer before the strike** (glass-ui **7.0.0 EXACT**, the ruled pin; read at the installed dist, never guessed): `DarkModeToggle` destructures `let { class: e, type: t, ...n } = h;` and spreads `n` onto **the same `<button>`** that already carries its own state-aware `aria-label: v.value ? "Switch to light mode" : "Switch to dark mode"` plus `aria-pressed`. A `title` therefore lands on the named element itself and ships a **visible string that is not in the accessible name** — WCAG 2.5.3 Label in Name. In the menu the adjacent `Dark mode` span is already the row's visible label; in the ribbon the control is icon-only and the producer's own name is the correct one. **No tooltip is substituted** — that would be a second, demo-owned name competing with the producer's.

#### §2 · EH-5 ⊕ EH-8 — ONE sizing decision across the ribbon cluster

**`fe6e5dfa`** — the brief requires these **decided together**, and they are: one commit, one rule, three controls.

**Producer facts, measured at 7.0.0 before deciding** (each read at the dist): `--dark-mode-toggle-size: 2.25rem` is the base, with `sm` / `lg` / `control` / `dock` arms and **no `md` arm**; `.button[data-icon-only] { inline-size: var(--button-size); block-size: var(--button-size) }`; `--control-h-sm: 2.25rem`, `--control-h-md: 2.5rem`.

The defect those facts expose: `class="aspect-square w-8"` on an `icon-only` Button sets only the **inline** axis (utilities cascade after components), so the box was **32px × md** and the `aspect-ratio: 1` was **inert against two definite dimensions** — a width utility silently overriding a producer control's own geometry, and `aspect-square` reading as though it had fixed it. The cure is the `size` vocabulary: `size="sm"` → `--control-h-sm` `2.25rem`, **the same rung `DarkModeToggle`'s own base ships**, so the two chrome controls agree **by construction** rather than by a coincidence of literals. `.b` §2.8's ONE `min-block-size` policy ("no demo `h-*` on a glass CONTROL; height is `size`-driven") is consumed, not re-decided. **Rendered magnitudes are left to KF.W9**, as the brief directs — this commit decides the *vocabulary*, not the number.

#### §3 · KF-APP-5 — the CONSUMER half (supply `#anchor`)

**`f1324983`** — the row's producer half rides the BH relay (**O-26 R-11**); this is the consumer half only, and it is supplied by **using** the API rather than fighting the seam.

**The seam, read at the producer's render before writing a line**: `HeaderRibbon` renders its actions with `inert` + `aria-hidden` and **zero inline-size** until `expanded` (= `pinned || hovered || focus-within`), opens on hover **only for a non-touch pointer**, its anchor `div` carries `onClick: k` (the pin toggle), its root `onFocusin` sets focus-within, and Escape restores focus via `b.value?.querySelector("button, a, [tabindex]:not([tabindex='-1'])")`. **The `#anchor` slot was EMPTY.** With no focusable in it there was **no tab stop, no touch affordance and no Escape landing target**: Share and the theme toggle were keyboard- and AT-unreachable in the shell, and the shortcuts modal was wholly unreachable on touch (its only two routes are this ribbon and the `?` shortcut).

One real focusable control is added in the anchor — a quiet `icon-only` `size="sm"` Button with `:aria-pressed="pinned"` and a state-aware `aria-label` (`Unpin header actions` / `Show header actions`), `Pin`/`PinOff`. **It deliberately carries NO click handler**: the producer's own wrapper owns the click, and adding a second would double-toggle. Tab reaches it → focusin expands the band → the three actions leave `inert`; tap pins on touch, where hover never fires.

#### §4 · ChromeDock D-22 — delete the single-slot wrapper

**`412b8324`** — the `<div class="flex items-center gap-2">` around the dock row is deleted (opening tag and its `</div>`).

Two costs, the second consequential: it substituted `8px` for the token'd `--dock-layer-gap`; and it made itself **the single direct child of the slot**, so every onset step of the producer's staggered reveal — `.dock-layer.is-active > *:nth-child(2 of *)`, `:nth-child(3 of *)`, `:nth-child(n+4):nth-child(-n+5)` and their `nth-last-child` mirrors, **read at `dist/components/dock/styles/layers.css`** — matched that one div, and the whole row faded and scaled as a block. **The dock's signature expand animation was silently absent on the app's most-seen chrome.** The producer's `.dock-layer` already *is* the flex row (`display:flex; align-items:center; gap: var(--dock-layer-gap)`), so the wrapper bought nothing it did not also break. If `8px` is wanted it is `--dock-layer-gap`'s to say; the magnitude is KF.W9's.

#### §5 · ChromeDock D-5 (+ KF-APP-26) — ONE glyph rung, and it is the dock's own

**`4cf174eb`** — every dock glyph site moves off the demo's `icon-*` family onto the producer's own token.

Three defects, one root: the demo's `icon-{xs,sm,md,lg}` `@utility` family (14/16/20/24px) **shadows glass-ui's `--icon-*` scale** (12/14/16/20px) under identical stems, uniformly one rung up. Reading those stems inside the dock (a) put **four** glyph rungs in one row; (b) let `icon-lg` — an `@layer utilities` rule, therefore later than `@layer components` — **defeat** `.dock-icon-button > svg { width: var(--dock-icon-glyph) }`, drifting the dock's invariant 0.5 glyph-to-control ratio; and (c) inverted the file's own grammar (the **identity** glyph was the smallest thing in the row at `icon-sm`=16, the panel toggle the largest at `icon-lg`=24).

Cure: one scoped rule reading the producer's token, and **the DockControl glyphs lose `icon-lg` entirely** — the producer already sizes its own `> svg` from the same token, which is exactly the invariant `icon-lg` was defeating.

```css
.dock-glyph { width: var(--dock-icon-glyph); height: var(--dock-icon-glyph); }
```

`--dock-icon-glyph` = `max(--dock-layer-height * --dock-icon-glyph-ratio, --dock-icon-glyph-floor)` and is declared at `:root` **as well as** on `.glass-dock[data-size]`, so it resolves inside the **portalled** SelectContent too — checked, because a `.glass-dock`-scoped-only token would have silently failed there.

**KF-APP-26 rides in the same commit** (the KF-APP-25 token-alignment family): `.dock-inline-tab`'s `padding-inline: var(--dock-label-padding-inline, 0.5rem)` named a **phantom** — one consumer, **zero definitions** anywhere in the demo or the dist — so the rule had always been running on its own literal fallback while *looking* token-driven. Repointed to the producer's shipped `--dock-trigger-padding-inline`. The `icon-*` family's own corpus-wide disposition is W6-D's audit row, not this file's.

#### §6 · ChromeDock D-23 / C-5 — a GETTER, never a selector string

**`e64103be`** — `:background-canvas="auroraCanvas"` on `<GlassDock>`, where

```ts
const auroraCanvas = (): HTMLCanvasElement | null =>
    typeof document === "undefined"
        ? null
        : document.querySelector<HTMLCanvasElement>(".hero-aurora canvas");
```

**THE CURE LAW, proved at the bytes — both halves, because the two disagree.** `GlassDock` does **not** hand its prop to the resolver; it wraps it, verbatim at `dist/dock.js`:

```js
backgroundCanvas: () => { let e = n.backgroundCanvas;
  return typeof e == "function" ? e() : e instanceof HTMLCanvasElement ? e : null; }
```

A **string** falls through both arms and is `null` **before** the underlying resolver — which *does* handle selectors — ever sees it. The `.d.ts` advertises *"an element, a getter, or a CSS selector"*; the shipped adapter honours two of the three. **A selector-string diff would type-check, review clean, and change nothing.** Hence a getter, resolving through the **demo's own** `.hero-aurora` wrapper (`HeroAurora.vue`), never a producer-internal class — the standing law forbids a copied producer selector.

**Effect measured in-browser (bounded probe, §5.2)**: the dock's `data-backdrop-sample-reason` moves `source-unavailable` → **`sample-unavailable`**, and the two docks now differ **exactly in the leg this commit supplied** (ChromeDock `sample-unavailable` · TransportDock still `source-unavailable`) — corroboration the binding is live and reaching the sampler. **The residue is diagnosed, not papered over**: a `drawImage` readback of the aurora canvas measures `maxAlpha 0 / rgbSum 0` because the canvas is created with `preserveDrawingBuffer: false`. Aurora **publishes** `runtimeOptions.preserveDrawingBuffer?: boolean`, so the completing half is **ONE token in `HeroAurora.vue`** — which is **unit `.j`'s ONE aurora config motion, outside this unit's writable set**. Declared, routed, **not reached for**, and **no `node_modules` patch** was contemplated. Contrast delta and observer cost are KF.W9's.

#### §7 · ChromeDock D-24 — stop signalling SELECTION with a HEALTH primitive

**`7b721d10`** — `hide-indicator` and all three `<StatusDot …>` go, with the `StatusDot` import.

The defect in one line: a **health-status** primitive (`online` green / dashed `unknown` ring, **one per NON-current row**) was carrying the **selection** signal in three menus — against this dock's own "never a saturated brand hue" register — while the primitive **built for the job**, the producer's `SelectItem` **indicator**, was switched off at every one of those rows by `hide-indicator`. The demo hid the selection vocabulary and re-invented it out of a health vocabulary. Leaving the indicator on is enough: reka renders it for `data-state="checked"` **alone**, so the **current** row is marked instead of every **other** row being marked "unknown".

**Why `--select-dot-color` is bound explicitly — measured, not preferred.** The producer's dot paints `background-color: var(--select-dot-color, var(--glass-accent, currentColor))`, and **in this app the middle link is dead**: `--glass-accent: var(--color-gold)` is declared by the producer where `--color-gold` is not yet defined and the property is `@property`-registered, so it falls to its **transparent initial** — measured `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)` at both `:root` and `body`, while the demo's own `--color-gold` measures `#d9a520` one scope later. **Dropping `hide-indicator` alone would have swapped a wrong-but-visible signal for an INVISIBLE one.** The binding names the chain's own terminal link, `currentColor` — the row's ink, legible in both themes for free, and pointedly not a saturated brand hue.

**`--dock-selected-accent` is NOT what gets bound, and that is measured too**: its only consumer in the dist is `.glass-dock.vertical .dock-icon-button:is(.is-active, …)::before` — a **vertical rail** accent strip — resolving to a **14%** wash (`oklab(0.216 … / 0.14)`) sized for a 3px bar. Piping that into an 8px dot on a **horizontal** dock authors an invisible affordance. The row's premise that the token sits unused is true and stays true; at this dock's orientation it is unreachable. Recorded in source, not silently dropped.

**Delivery idiom, forced by measurement**: a `.dock-select-content` **scoped** rule does **not** apply — the portalled `SelectContent` root carries the producer's `data-v-f79fd526`, not ChromeDock's scope id. The binding is therefore an inline `:style`, which is also this file's own documented portal-safe idiom (the `--ppmycota-primary` precedent). **No `:deep` (banned), no global block.**

#### §8 · The MbabbMenu motion — MM-4 + MM-15 + MM-30 + MM-8/-9/-29/-31, ONE commit

**`3cc7e126`** — the family the brief names, landed whole. (The **MUST-CARRY rider travels wherever ChromeDock M-4 lands**; M-4 is **not** this unit's, so the rider does not land here and is **not** discharged here.)

- **MM-29 (site 1)** — `normal-case` cancels `text-transform` **and nothing else**: `text-mono-caption` also binds `letter-spacing: var(--type-tracking-caps)` (0.1em), which rode on regardless, so a lowercase handle shipped with **CAPS tracking**. `tracking-normal` is paired with it (G-W6-8 binds this wave and KF.W7 identically). The responsive rung pair is left exactly as authored — collapsing it would decide a dock-band **magnitude**, and magnitudes are KF.W9's.
- **MM-15** — `z-popover`, not `z-modal`. The demo's written z-contract reserves `--z-modal` (140) for modal dialogs. **The wrong rung was INERT and therefore invisible**: the producer's **unlayered** `z-index: var(--z-popover)` outranked the layered utility, so both portalled siblings sat at 130 and DOM order decided. The moment glass layers its sheet (MM-4, relayed as O-26 R-1) **the utility ARMS** — and `z-modal` would then occlude the Share popover. Corrected **before** the producer fix lands, which is precisely why it rides this motion.
- **MM-4 (interim, and labelled as one in source)** — glass-ui emits `.dropdown-menu__item` **outside** its single `@layer components` block (**byte 29523** against the block's **2531–18827**), and an unlayered rule beats a layered one whatever the source order — so `cursor-pointer` on a row that has an `@click` **never paints** and the row reads as inert. The durable fix is the producer's and was **sent as O-26 R-1 before this interim was written** (§Sequencing 7). Until it lands the paint is declared inline — the file's own portal-safe idiom — **with the class kept alongside so that removing the `:style` is the whole migration**. `MM-44`: this file is the demo's **only** DropdownMenu consumer, so the interim's blast radius is this component.
- **MM-30** — the row reached for the **wrong red**. `text-destructive` is the vendor's `--destructive`, while this repo's own law (`style.css:114-119`, which names Clear-all **by name**) assigns `--accent-red` — and `--accent-red` alone carries a bespoke `.dark` arm (`style.css:187`) and a Tailwind bridge (`--color-accent-red`, `style.css:58`). **Order matters**: curing MM-4's layer first would have *armed* the class and painted the vendor's red, so the token correction had to land **with** it.
- **MM-8 + MM-9** — `text-admin-label` is a 10px **all-caps mono chip** register (`font-mono` · `text-transform: uppercase` · caps tracking · `line-height: 1`) and it was carrying **four sentence-case English sentences**: wrong register for prose (MM-8) and a T.D4 mono-contract breach (MM-9, none of these leaves is a `monoAllowedSelectors` entry) in one place. All four move to `text-micro` (11px, inherited text face, no transform, no caps tracking). **The mono register survives at the one leaf that earns it** — the bare `ppmycota.com` URL, an artifact string, not prose.
- **MM-31 (+ MM-29 site 2, cured STRUCTURALLY rather than patched)** — the `@mbabb` title was simultaneously the **smallest** string in the menu and its **only bold** one: the person the menu is named after read below "Share" and "Clear all & reload". It takes its siblings' rung (`text-mono-small` = `--type-small`, the same size token the sibling titles read; mono is correct because `@mbabb` is a case-significant identifier, `data-register="code"`), and the bold goes with the inversion. **MM-29 needs no pairing here** because `text-mono-small` applies neither `text-transform` nor `--type-tracking-caps` — the `normal-case` that used to sit there existed only to cancel the caption rung's transform, and the right rung never had one.

A consumer-side re-bake from `AmigaScene.vue` was considered and REJECTED rather than taken: it would have to restate that file's two colour constants at a second site — the restatement/divergence class this wave convicts one file over. **Owner: seat 0 / KF.W10. The cure needs no bounds WIDENING beyond naming one file already inside the wave's own scene surface.**
2. **`CubeScene D-7 ≡ CubeTarget #9` (the face palette) and `CubeTarget #58 ≡ KF-AX-3` (the axis triple).** Both cure at TOKEN DEFINITIONS in `demo/styles/style.css` ⟨`git grep -n -- '--face-[1-6]' -- demo/`⟩ → `style.css:154-159`; ⟨`git grep -n -- '--axis-[wxyz]' -- demo/`⟩ → `style.css:115-118`. **`style.css` IS a §Bounds file of this wave** (spec `:62`, access `modify`) but is **not in `.g`'s writable set** — it is `.h`'s and `.l`'s. A per-face or per-axis override at the consumer would be the masking this wave exists to catch, and re-cutting a crayon at a call site would break the hoist's own hue-exactness guarantee. **Cure needs NO bounds widening — only a different partition of the SAME §Bounds: re-home both rows to a seat holding `style.css` and keep them in ONE motion with `#8`.**
3. **`CubeTarget #8` — and the missing register member, which is the sharpest finding of this unit.** The veil mixes `--background`, so in the DEFAULT light arm an away face renders BRIGHTER than a lit one. #### §4 · KF-HA-2 — the consumer decision, and why the halved ceiling is DECLINED

**The question the wave owns is "which number did the owner bless?", and the answer is the DELIVERED 0.1** — the OD-2 amendment's own bound, already encoded and already asserted.

The banked ~0.19 is REAL **and its scope is one artifact**. M1/M2/M7 measure this tree's installed dist: the ceiling is a custom property on the root that **both** children read as their own element opacity, the placeholder is never hidden, and the installed `.d.ts` documents that stacking in its own words — so two layers of the same field at `c` compose to `1-(1-c)²`, i.e. **0.19 at c = 0.1**. M3 measures the PUBLISHED v7.0.0 at the producer's own tag: the ceiling is applied **ONCE, on the shared root**, the placeholder carries no opacity, and the arm cross-fade lives on a layer wrapper our dist does not contain. **I-35 R-4 says the same thing from the producer's end** and adds the part that settles it — `opacityCeiling` IS the API, the owner's blessed number belongs there, hide-on-arm is DECLINED permanently (the ground is the surface a failed runtime falls back to), and nothing retires the envelope.

So halving to **0.05** would tune the hero against **a pre-release artifact sitting in one tree's `node_modules`** and ship a half-presence wash to every clean install — the deploy-truth defect class this tranche exists to catch. **The literal does not move; the FRONTIER LOCK does not fire, and `test/demo/instrument/aurora-opacity-ceiling.test.ts` is NOT touched, loosened or skipped** (it stays green on its own terms: `0 < 0.1 < 0.15` and the mount binds it).

**What this DOES surface is a substrate finding, and it is not this unit's to cure**: our installed 7.0.0 is not the published 7.0.0 — different compositing shape, different scope attribute (`data-v-2d1b9db2` vs the `data-v-aebed9bd` the producer cites), `aurora` 12× vs their 10×. That is I-35 §0's finding measured independently from our end, and it is already **ESCALATED to seat 0 / KF.W0** by the third dispatch's own ruling. `node_modules` is in no unit's writable set and **nothing was re-installed**.

**KF-HA-8's radius half, stated so it is not read as skipped**: `setCursorRadius` is exposed and still uncalled, i.e. the axis runs at the library's 0.25. Calling it would spend an invented number on a magnitude only a rendered viewport can judge (SS-13 P-1), so the decision is *examined and kept at the producer's default*, written at the bytes — the same shape `.e` used for every RETAINED-BY-POLICY row.

The interesting defect is a duplicated idiom, not a height utility. **Neither cure is this seat's, and the reason is the finding**: adopting the class does not satisfy §2.8 either, because `.btn-playback` sets `height` on a glass control too — the violation would only move into a shared file — while striking the height outright leaves Re-seat at the producer's `md` rung beside a 2rem sibling and visibly breaks the ribbon. The subject is the whole shared idiom (`PlaybackRibbon.vue` and the easing scene are its other consumers) and its file is out of bounds. Measured for the taker: the published rungs are `--control-h-xs` 1.75rem · `-sm` 2.25rem · `-md` 2.5rem · `-lg` 2.75rem, so **2rem names no rung** and the ask is a producer rung, never a demo height. **Also corrected in passing**: §2.1's exemplar citation (*"the shipping 32px answer, `SharePopover.vue`, `class="h-8 w-8 p-0"`"*) no longer reproduces — SP-8/SP-9 cured it, and those buttons are now `size="sm" emphasis="quiet" icon-only` with no height override.
